3609: "The human paladin stood unwavering as the horde approached, her armor gleaming with divine light. With a thunderous proclamation of her oath, she raised her sword skyward. Radiance cascaded down the blade as she brought it crashing into the earth, sending a wave of holy energy outward that caused the undead to shriek and recoil. Her allies, emboldened by her presence, charged forward with renewed vigor, protected by the aura of divine power that emanated from her very being."
3610: 
3611: Paladins are sacred warriors bound by divine Oath, living expressions of conviction that shape both the battlefield and the world around them. In Aethel, these champions represent the ultimate fusion of martial prowess and divine power, their strength drawn not from study or innate talent, but from the unbreakable vows they swear and uphold.
3612: 
3613: Class Overview
3614: 
3615: As a paladin, you are defined by the Oath you take and the conviction with which you uphold it. Your divine power manifests through your Smite attacks, protective Auras, and Revelation spells, all shaped by the nature of your sacred vow. Beyond your combat abilities, you serve as a beacon of your Oath's ideals, inspiring allies and striking fear into those who oppose your principles.
3616: 
3617: In Aethel, paladins might serve as champions of religious orders, guardians of sacred sites, enforcers of divine law, or wandering knights seeking to spread their Oath's influence. Their rigid moral codes and divine powers make them respected, feared, or both, depending on the nature of their Oath and how they choose to uphold it.
3618: 
3619: Core Mechanics
3620: 
3621: The Oath – At Level 3, you swear an Oath that defines your paladin's path, granting you a specific Smite Type, Aura Effect, Signature Power, Revelation Spell List, and Conduct Clause. Violating your Oath's tenets risks falling to the Oathbreaker path, corrupting your divine powers.
3622: 
3623: Smite System – Divine-empowered melee strikes that evolve based on your Oath, dealing additional damage and applying special effects.
3624: 
3625: Aura System – Passive zone effects that emanate from you, strengthening with level and providing benefits based on your Oath type.
3626: 
3627: Lay on Hands – A healing pool equal to 5 × your Paladin level that can be used to restore health or remove conditions.
3628: 
3629: Primary Attributes
3630: Main Attribute: Charisma (for auras, Smite, saves, spell DCs)
3631: Secondary Attributes: Strength or Constitution (based on build)
3632: Hit Points: High (10 + Constitution modifier at 1st level, 1d10 per level thereafter)
3633: Armor Proficiency: All armor types and shields
3634: Weapon Proficiency: All simple and martial weapons
3635: Tool Proficiency: None
3636: 
3637: Progression Path
3638: 
3639: Level 1-2 (Base Paladin Skills)
3640: Foundational skills before Oath selection.
3641: 
3642: Passive Skills
3643: Lay on Hands: Healing pool equal to 5 × your Paladin level; can heal or remove conditions.
3644: Divine Sense: Detect the presence of celestial, fiendish, or undead entities within 60 feet.
3645: Martial Proficiency: Proficiency with all armor types, shields, and weapons.
3646: 
3647: Active Skills
3648: Divine Strike: Channel divine energy into your weapon attacks, dealing additional damage.
3649: Protective Stance: Enter a defensive posture that increases your AC and allows you to intercept attacks meant for nearby allies.
3650: Righteous Challenge: Mark an enemy, forcing them to focus on you or suffer penalties.
3651: Bolstering Presence: Inspire nearby allies, granting them temporary bonuses to saves against fear and charm effects.
3652: Sacred Touch: Channel divine energy to stabilize a dying creature or detect the presence of curses and diseases.
3653: 
3654: Level 3-9 (Oath Selection and Development)
3655: At level 3, paladins swear an Oath, gaining access to Oath-specific abilities and spells.
3656: 
3657: Oath Selection (Choose One at Level 3)
3658: Oath of Mercy: Protection, healing, and preservation of life
3659: Oath of Vengeance: Fury, execution, and relentless pursuit of enemies
3660: Oath of Dominion: Authority, law, and divine rulership
3661: Oath of Flame: Purification, zeal, and cleansing fire
3662: Oath of the Ancients: Nature, memory, and primordial power
3663: Oath of Silence: Humility, anti-pride, and quiet strength
3664: Oath of the Chain: Martyrdom, warding, and protective bonds
3665: Oath of Grief: Sorrow, funerary power, and memorialization
3666: Oath of Chains Broken: Liberty, rebellion, and freedom
3667: Oath of the Martyr King: Kingship, burden, and noble sacrifice
3668: 
3669: Oath-Granted Abilities
3670: Smite Type: Determines the energy type and effects of your Smite attacks
3671: Aura Effect: A constant passive zone that strengthens with level
3672: Revelation Spell List: 5-6 custom spells drawn from your Oath's domain
3673: Conduct Clause: Roleplay-binding rules you must follow or risk falling
3674: 
3675: Aura Scaling
3676: Level 3: 10 ft radius, base aura effect active while conscious
3677: Level 6: 15 ft radius, gains secondary benefit (e.g., DR, saves)
3678: Level 7: 15 ft radius, gain reactive Smite or spell trigger
3679: Level 10: 20 ft radius, aura persists for 1 round if incapacitated
3680: Level 15: 30 ft radius, aura debuffs enemies or buffs allies
3681: Level 17: 30 ft radius, Aura Surge (1/rest) - double aura power or trigger burst effect
3682: Level 20: 30+ ft radius, Divine Manifestation - aura auto-activates on initiative, cannot be suppressed
3683: 
3684: Level 10+ (Oath Mastery)
3685: As you advance, your connection to your Oath deepens, granting more powerful abilities.
3686: 
3687: Signature Power (Level 11)
3688: Gain access to your Oath's Signature Power, a potent once-per-rest divine ability unique to your chosen Oath:
3689: 
3690: Mercy: Miracle Veil – Heal + Prevent death
3691: Vengeance: Judgment Brand – Doom mark, bonus crit
3692: Dominion: Heaven's Decree – Obey or suffer
3693: Flame: Holy Inferno – AoE Smite purge
3694: Ancients: Thorn Crown – Reflect status
3695: Silence: Vow Unbroken – Bonus Smite if you stay silent
3696: Chain: Warden's Lock – Lock enemies in zone
3697: Grief: Final Vigil – Channel ally's power
3698: Chains Broken: Righteous Quake – Break bindings, AoE push
3699: Martyr King: Crown of Embers – Team-wide DR + damage redirection
3700: 
3701: Divine Resistance (Level 13)
3702: Choose one type of damage to gain resistance against: Fire, Necrotic, or Psychic.
3703: 
3704: Lay on Hands: Full Cleansing Wave (Level 17)
3705: Once per rest, expend your entire Lay on Hands pool to create a wave of healing energy that affects all allies within your aura radius.
3706: 
3707: Divine Sense: Detect Disguised Outsiders (Level 18)
3708: Your Divine Sense now reveals outsiders (celestials, fiends, etc.) even if they are disguised or shapeshifted.
3709: 
3710: Final Oath Gift (Level 20)
3711: Receive the ultimate blessing of your Oath, a powerful capstone ability that represents the pinnacle of your divine connection.
3712: 
3713: The Oathbreaker Path
3714: 
3715: When a paladin violates their Oath's Conduct Clause, they risk falling to the Oathbreaker path. This corruption inverts their divine powers, replacing them with darker versions:
3716: 
3717: Death Touch: Replaces Lay on Hands with a harmful touch ability
3718: Aura of Dread: Inverts your aura's effects to harm or hinder
3719: Blasphemous Word: A corrupted version of your Signature Power
3720: Forbidden Spell List: Dark spells that replace your Revelation list
3721: 
3722: Each Oath has a corresponding Oathbreaker path:
3723: 
3724: Mercy → Cruelty: Life Drain AoE, healing reduced 50%, Exsanguinate
3725: Vengeance → Malice: Execute chance, increased damage to wounded, Fearbrand
3726: Dominion → Tyranny: Will control, psychic damage for disobedience, Obey
3727: Flame → Inferno: Burn healed enemies, remove buffs and block regeneration, Scorch the Sanctified
3728: Ancients → Withering Grove: Blight terrain, remove regeneration and resistance, Lifeleech Bloom
3729: Silence → Silent Heretic: Silence plus psychic damage, prevent verbal casting, Null Command
3730: Chain → Shackled Lord: Steal reactions, slow and control debuffs, Iron Leash
3731: Grief → Gravetongue: Necrotic plus grief effects, block resurrection and healing, Dirge of Ruin
3732: Chains Broken → Unbound Flame: Burn and charm effects, prevent buffs and escape, Reign of Ashes
3733: Martyr King → Usurper's Crown: Steal passive powers, damage requirement for healing, Crownshatter Decree
3734: 
3735: Example Oath: Mercy
3736: 
3737: Theme: Protection, healing, and preservation of life
3738: 
3739: Smite Type: Radiant - Deals additional radiant damage and heals nearby allies for a portion of the damage dealt
3740: 
3741: Aura Effect: Aura of Mercy - Allies within your aura gain increased healing received and resistance to necrotic damage
3742: 
3743: Signature Power: Miracle Veil - Create a protective barrier around an ally that prevents them from dropping below 1 HP for the duration
3744: 
3745: Conduct Clause:
3746: You must never refuse a genuine plea for healing or protection
3747: You must prioritize saving lives over taking them
3748: You must show mercy to defeated foes when possible
3749: You must not use lethal force against the innocent or defenseless
3750: 
3751: Revelation Spell List:
3752: Level 3: Blessing of the Meek – Heal + AC if HP < 50%
3753: Level 5: Ward of Shelter – Allies gain +2 DR, resist fear
3754: Level 9: Channel Life – Transfer HP from self to ally
3755: Level 13: Sanctify Ground – Zone that removes conditions each round
3756: Level 17: Grace Reversal – Revive fallen ally if you fall to 0 HP
3757: 
3758: Oathbreaker: Cruelty
3759: If you violate your Oath of Mercy, you fall to the path of Cruelty:
3760: 
3761: Death Touch: Life Drain AoE - Steal life from all creatures around you
3762: Aura of Dread: Healing Suppression - All healing within your aura is reduced by 50%
3763: Blasphemous Word: Exsanguinate - Drain life from all creatures in a large radius, healing yourself for the damage dealt
3764: Forbidden Spell List: Spells focused on pain, suffering, and preventing healing
3765: 
3766: Playing a Paladin
3767: 
3768: As a paladin in Decennium Descent, your effectiveness depends on upholding your Oath, managing your divine resources, and strategically deploying your aura and Smite abilities. Unlike other classes that might have more flexibility, you excel at embodying your Oath's principles and drawing power from your unwavering conviction.
3769: 
3770: Combat Strategy
3771: Your approach to combat will vary based on your chosen Oath:
3772: 
3773: Mercy Paladins focus on protection and healing, using their aura to enhance allies' survivability while their Smite provides both damage and healing.
3774: 
3775: Vengeance Paladins excel at single-target elimination, marking enemies for destruction and pursuing them relentlessly across the battlefield.
3776: 
3777: Dominion Paladins control the battlefield through authority and divine law, forcing enemies to submit or suffer consequences.
3778: 
3779: All paladins should remember that their Oath is not just a source of power but a binding contract, violating its tenets risks corruption and transformation into an Oathbreaker.
3780: 
3781: Reputation and Social Consequences
3782: Your choice of Oath significantly affects how you're perceived in the world of Aethel:
3783: 
3784: Mercy, Ancients, and Chain Paladins are generally welcomed in most communities for their protective and beneficial natures.
3785: 
3786: Dominion, Flame, and Martyr King Paladins are respected but sometimes feared for their authoritative presence and zealous dedication.
3787: 
3788: Vengeance, Grief, and Chains Broken Paladins may face suspicion in peaceful areas but are sought after in times of conflict or oppression.
3789: 
3790: Oathbreakers of any type are universally feared and mistrusted, their corrupted divine power marking them as dangerous and unpredictable.
3791: 
3792: Notable Paladin NPCs in Aethel
3793: 
3794: Lady Seraphina Lightbringer
3795: A human Oath of Mercy paladin who leads the Order of the Healing Hand. Her miraculous healing abilities have saved countless lives, and her aura brings comfort to the sick and wounded. She travels to areas ravaged by war or disease, establishing sanctuaries where all are welcome regardless of faction or background.
3796: 
3797: Commander Thorne Ironheart
3798: A dwarf Oath of Dominion paladin who serves as the chief enforcer of Imperial law in the frontier regions. His unwavering dedication to order has brought stability to previously lawless territories, though some find his methods harsh and unyielding. His aura compels truth-telling, making his court a place where deception is impossible.
3799: 
3800: The Ashen Knight
3801: A mysterious Oath of Flame paladin whose face is never seen beneath their helm. They appear at sites of corruption or undead infestation, purging the taint with holy fire before disappearing as suddenly as they arrived. Some whisper that the Knight was once corrupted themselves and now seeks redemption through their zealous purification crusade.
3802: 
3803: Ranger
3804: 
3805: "The elven ranger moved silently through the underbrush, her bow drawn and ready. Three bandits sat around their campfire, unaware of her presence. In one fluid motion, she nocked an arrow imbued with crackling frost, loosed it at the first target, then seamlessly drew her twin blades as she leapt into the clearing. The arrow struck true, ice spreading across the first bandit's chest as she engaged the others in close combat, her movements a deadly dance between blade and bow."
3806: 
3807: Rangers are versatile skirmishers who blend precision archery, dual-wielded melee combat, and nature magic. In the world of Aethel, these wilderness experts thrive in terrain others fear, excelling at tracking, terrain control, and hybrid combat styles that allow them to adapt to any situation.
3808: 
3809: Class Overview
3810: 
3811: As a ranger, you harness both martial prowess and natural magic, moving fluidly between ranged and melee combat while using the environment to your advantage. Your specialty lies in tracking specific targets, imbuing your weapons with elemental power, and executing devastating combo finishers that blend weapon strikes with druidic magic.
3812: 
3813: In Aethel, rangers might serve as frontier scouts, wilderness guides, monster hunters, or guardians of ancient groves. Their intimate knowledge of the land and versatile combat abilities make them invaluable allies in the untamed regions beyond civilization's reach.
3814: 
3815: Core Mechanics
3816: 
3817: Track Target – Choose one enemy to mark as your quarry. Tracked enemies take +10% damage from your attacks and enable special abilities like Spirit Fang Arrow.
3818: 
3819: Weapon Synergy – Gain bonuses when switching between ranged and melee combat:
3820: Melee → Ranged: Next ranged attack gains +10% crit chance
3821: Ranged → Melee: Next melee attack causes Bleed for 2 rounds
3822: 
3823: Elemental Imbue – Infuse your weapons with fire (Burn), cold (Slow), or poison (Weaken) to deal additional elemental damage and effects.
3824: 
3825: Druidic Arrow Combo – After landing 3 successful weapon attacks, unleash an enhanced Druidic Arrow spell with powerful effects based on your combat style and environment.
3826: 
3827: Primary Attributes
3828: Main Attribute: Dexterity
3829: Secondary Attribute: Wisdom
3830: Hit Points: Medium (8 + Constitution modifier at 1st level, 1d8 per level thereafter)
3831: Armor Proficiency: Light and medium armor (no plate)
3832: Weapon Proficiency: Longbows, shortbows, spears, dual-wielded blades
3833: Tool Proficiency: Survival tools, herbalism kit
3834: 
3835: Progression Path
3836: 
3837: Level 1-2 (Base Ranger Skills)
3838: Foundational skills before Arch class selection.
3839: 
3840: Passive Skills
3841: Weapon Bond: Form a special connection with either a bow or a pair of blades, allowing you to summon them to your hand.
3842: Track Target: Mark an enemy to deal +10% damage and enable special abilities against them.
3843: Natural Explorer: Gain advantage on Survival checks and move normally through difficult natural terrain.
3844: Keen Senses: Gain enhanced perception in natural environments, making it harder for creatures to hide from you.
3845: Combat Focus: Choose one combat style (Precision, Momentum, or Wild Hunt) that enhances your fighting abilities.
3846: 
3847: Active Skills
3848: Quick Shot: Fire a rapid arrow that deals less damage but can be used as part of another action.
3849: Twin Strike: Make a swift attack with both melee weapons when dual wielding.
3850: Nature's Whisper: Cast a minor nature spell that reveals hidden creatures or tracks within a small area.
3851: Skirmisher's Step: Move half your speed without provoking opportunity attacks.
3852: Elemental Attunement: Temporarily attune yourself to an element (fire, cold, or poison), gaining minor resistance and the ability to detect that element.
3853: 
3854: Level 3-9 (Arch Class: Pathfinder)
3855: At level 3, rangers specialize into the Pathfinder arch class, enhancing their mobility and awareness.
3856: 
3857: Passive Skills
3858: Ignore Difficult Terrain: Move normally through any type of difficult terrain.
3859: Enhanced Stealth and Perception: Gain +2 to Stealth and Perception checks.
3860: Tactical Draw: Gain +1 AC after switching from bow to blade or vice versa.
3861: Vigilant Heart: Become immune to surprise and scrying attempts.
3862: Dual Style: Gain the ability to swap weapons as a free action (level 5).
3863: Elemental Imbue: Infuse your weapons with fire, cold, or poison to deal additional effects (level 6).
3864: 
3865: Active Skills
3866: Pathfinder's Mark: Place a magical mark on a location that you can sense the direction and distance to for 24 hours.
3867: Natural Camouflage: Blend into natural surroundings, gaining advantage on Stealth checks while stationary.
3868: Terrain Analysis: Quickly survey an area to gain tactical information about the environment.
3869: Swift Traverse: Move at double speed for a short time when in natural terrain.
3870: Predator's Mark: Mark a tracked target for bonus damage and enhanced awareness of their movements (level 9).
3871: 
3872: Level 10+ (Hero Path Selection)
3873: At level 10, rangers choose a Hero Path that reflects their philosophy and methods, further specializing their abilities.
3874: 
3875: Warder (Hero Path)
3876: "The land provides for those who protect it."
3877: 
3878: Passive Skills
3879: Living Pulse: Regenerate 2 HP per round while in natural terrain.
3880: Beast Empathy: Communicate basic concepts with natural beasts.
3881: Warden's Vigilance: Detect threats to natural areas within a large radius.
3882: Healing Touch: Your healing spells and items are 25% more effective on allies.
3883: 
3884: Active Skills
3885: Rootcall Quake: Create an area effect that roots enemies in place.
3886: Healing Briar: Establish a healing zone that restores health to allies within it.
3887: Warden's Step: Teleport to an injured ally and taunt nearby foes.
3888: Nature's Blessing: Grant an ally temporary bonuses to speed and resilience.
3889: Beast Companion: Tame one beast from the bestiary (CR ≤ your level, INT < 7, not corrupted) as a permanent companion that scales with your Wisdom and level.
3890: 
3891: Stalker (Hero Path)
3892: "They never see me coming. They never see me leave."
3893: 
3894: Passive Skills
3895: Hunter's Reflex: Gain +10% Dodge chance after making a ranged attack.
3896: Shadow Step: Leave no tracks and make no sound when moving at normal speed.
3897: Keen Hunter: Tracked targets cannot hide from you within your line of sight.
3898: Efficient Killer: Reduce the Hit Chain requirement for Druidic Arrow to 2 hits instead of 3.
3899: 
3900: Active Skills
3901: Camouflage Step: Become invisible until you make your first attack.
3902: Silent Volley: Fire three arrows in rapid succession, ignoring armor bonuses.
3903: Shattershot: Fire a specialized arrow that breaks shields and removes magical buffs.
3904: Ghost Strike: Make a melee attack that bypasses physical armor.
3905: Vanishing Act: Disappear from sight and reposition to any point within 30 feet.
3906: 
3907: Outrider (Hero Path)
3908: "The land will rot. The prey will fall. All things return to the soil."
3909: 
3910: Passive Skills
3911: Predator's Grin: Killing a tracked target grants you an extra action once per rest.
3912: Toxic Affinity: Gain immunity to poison and disease.
3913: Corruption Touch: Your elemental imbues last twice as long and spread to adjacent targets on critical hits.
3914: Blight Infusion: Your Druidic Arrows inflict Blight on hit if the terrain is corrupted.
3915: 
3916: Active Skills
3917: Venom Rain: Create an area effect that deals poison damage and applies Corruption.
3918: Rotting Tether: Link an enemy to corrupted terrain, causing damage when they move.
3919: Entropic March: Convert natural terrain into Blighted terrain in a large area.
3920: Withering Shot: Fire an arrow that drastically reduces the target's healing received.
3921: Plague Bearer: Infect yourself with a controlled disease that spreads to enemies you damage.
3922: 
3923: Druidic Arrow Combo System
3924: 
3925: Starting at level 11, rangers generate a Hit Chain by landing 3 successful weapon attacks (ranged or melee). When the chain is complete, your next Druidic Arrow spell becomes free and enhanced. Casting the arrow consumes the combo and resets the chain.
3926: 
3927: Sample Druidic Arrows
3928: 
3929: | Name | Trigger | Effect |
3930: |------|---------|--------|
3931: | Emberroot Arrow | 3x Fire imbue hits | Fire + Burn + minor AoE explosion |
3932: | Glacial Thorn Arrow | 2 ranged, 1 melee | Cold + Root (2 rounds) |
3933: | Venomlash Arrow | Poison terrain | Poison + Weaken (–1 damage for 1 round) |
3934: | Spirit Fang Arrow | Target is Tracked | Crit chance doubled, bypass 50% DR |
3935: | Cleansing Grove Arrow | Natural terrain | AoE cleanse 1 condition from allies near target |
3936: 
3937: Each Hero path gains additional specialized Druidic Arrows:
3938: Warder: Nature's Wrath Arrow - Heals allies and damages enemies in the impact area
3939: Stalker: Phantom Arrow - Passes through the first target to hit a second
3940: Outrider: Corruption Arrow - Creates a zone of Blight that damages over time
3941: 
3942: Playing a Ranger
3943: 
3944: As a ranger in Decennium Descent, your effectiveness depends on mobility, target selection, and smooth transitioning between combat styles. Unlike pure martial or casting classes, you excel at adapting to changing battlefield conditions and using the environment to your advantage.
3945: 
3946: Combat Strategy
3947: Your approach to combat will vary based on your chosen Hero path:
3948: 
3949: Warder Rangers should focus on controlling the battlefield with roots and zone effects while using their Beast Companion to protect allies and disrupt enemy formations.
3950: 
3951: Stalker Rangers excel at eliminating high-value targets through stealth and precision, using their enhanced mobility to strike from unexpected angles and vanish before retaliation.
3952: 
3953: Outrider Rangers dominate through terrain corruption and poison effects, turning the environment itself into a weapon and wearing down enemies through sustained damage over time.
3954: 
3955: All rangers should remember that their strength lies in versatility, don't hesitate to switch between ranged and melee combat to maintain your weapon synergy bonuses and build your Hit Chain for powerful Druidic Arrow finishers.
3956: 
3957: Reputation and Social Consequences
3958: Your choice of Hero path significantly affects how you're perceived in the world of Aethel:
3959: 
3960: Warder Rangers are welcomed in most civilized areas and revered by druidic circles and nature-focused communities for their protective stance toward the natural world.
3961: 
3962: Stalker Rangers often maintain a neutral reputation, hired for their tracking and hunting skills without judgment. Many operate as mercenaries or bounty hunters.
3963: 
3964: Outrider Rangers are feared and mistrusted in most regions, their corruption of the land making them enemies of druids and nature spirits. They often find acceptance only among those who share their destructive goals.
3965: 
3966: Notable Ranger NPCs in Aethel
3967: 
3968: Thorne Oakshield
3969: A human Warder who protects the northern forests from encroaching corruption. His wolf companion, Silverfang, is known to scout ahead and warn travelers of dangers. Thorne maintains a network of Warders who monitor the health of the forest and intervene when necessary to maintain the balance.
3970: 
3971: Lyra Shadowstep
3972: An elven Stalker renowned for her ability to track any quarry across any terrain. She serves as a bounty hunter for the Imperial Court, bringing in high-value targets alive when possible. Her signature move, firing three arrows simultaneously at different targets, has become legendary among those who have witnessed it.
3973: 
3974: Malakai the Witherer
3975: A Umbrin Outrider who was exiled from his subterranean home for experiments with surface plant corruption. He now wanders the borderlands, creating pockets of blighted terrain as part of a grand experiment to develop new toxins. Rumors suggest he's developing a poison capable of corrupting even the ancient World Trees.
3976: 
3977: Shaman
3978: 
3979: "The orc shaman raised her gnarled staff, adorned with feathers and bones, as the enemy warriors approached. With a guttural chant, she slammed the staff into the earth, summoning a totem of flame that erupted from the ground. Fire spiraled around her allies' weapons as she reached into her pouch, pulling out a glowing flask. With practiced precision, she hurled it into the midst of the charging enemies, where it exploded in a shower of frost that slowed their advance to a crawl."
3980: 
3981: Shamans are primal mystics who channel elemental forces, craft volatile flasks, and deploy totems of spiritual power to dominate the battlefield. In the world of Aethel, these spiritual conduits draw their power not from gods but from the bones of the world itself, mixing blood, breath, and spirit into devastating concoctions and curses.
3982: 
3983: Class Overview
3984: 
3985: As a shaman, you harness the raw elemental forces of nature through a combination of spiritual connection and alchemical knowledge. Your specialty lies in controlling the battlefield through strategically placed totems, unleashing volatile elemental bombs, and channeling the spirits of the elements themselves. Beyond your combat abilities, you serve as a bridge between the material world and the realm of spirits, capable of communing with ancestors and elemental forces that others cannot perceive.
3986: 
3987: In Aethel, shamans might serve as tribal advisors, elemental wardens, spirit guides, or wandering mystics. Their connection to primal forces makes them both respected and sometimes feared, particularly in regions where more formalized religious structures dominate.
3988: 
3989: Core Mechanics
3990: 
3991: Totem System – Place powerful totems on the battlefield that create aura effects benefiting allies or hindering enemies. These persistent structures form the backbone of your tactical options.
3992: 
3993: Elemental Affinity – Choose an elemental attunement (Fire, Ice, Air, or Earth) that enhances your abilities and determines the effects of your totems and flasks.
3994: 
3995: Flask Bomb System – For Theurgists and Soothsayers, create and throw volatile elemental bombs as bonus actions, causing various effects from damage to healing.
3996: 
3997: Primary Attributes
3998: Main Attribute: Wisdom
3999: Secondary Attributes: Constitution, Intelligence
4000: Hit Points: Medium (8 + Constitution modifier at 1st level, 1d8 per level thereafter)
4001: Armor Proficiency: Light armor (leathers, bone, hide)
4002: Weapon Proficiency: Clubs, spears, staves (simple weapons only)
4003: Tool Proficiency: Alchemist's supplies, herbalism kit
4004: 
4005: Progression Path
4006: 
4007: Level 1-2 (Base Shaman Skills)
4008: Foundational skills before Arch class selection.
4009: 
4010: Passive Skills
4011: Elemental Affinity: Choose an element (Fire, Ice, Air, or Earth) that enhances your abilities.
4012: Spirit Sight: Perceive spirits and elemental forces invisible to others.
4013: Alchemical Knowledge: Identify potions and alchemical substances with greater accuracy.
4014: Primal Resilience: Gain resistance to your chosen elemental affinity.
4015: Ancestral Whispers: Occasionally receive guidance from ancestral spirits when in danger.
4016: 
4017: Active Skills
4018: Flask Use: Create and throw elemental bombs (2 per encounter) that cause various effects.
4019: Totem Casting: Place a totem that creates an aura effect for 3 rounds.
4020: Spirit Shock: Channel elemental energy into a damaging burst.
4021: Commune with Spirits: Gain information about an area or object by consulting with local spirits.
4022: Elemental Ward: Create a temporary barrier against a specific type of elemental damage.
4023: 
4024: Level 3-9 (Arch Class: Spiritwalker)
4025: At level 3, shamans specialize into the Spiritwalker arch class, deepening their connection to elemental forces.
4026: 
4027: Passive Skills
4028: Enhanced Elemental Attunement: Your chosen element grants additional benefits to your abilities.
4029: Extended Totem Duration: Totems last for an additional round.
4030: Spirit Cache: Store one flask or totem effect to recast later (once per encounter).
4031: Totem Pulse: Totems create an immediate area effect when placed (level 6).
4032: Spirit Saturation: Gain one free totem cast per combat (level 9).
4033: 
4034: Active Skills
4035: Elemental Channeling: Temporarily embody your chosen element, gaining related benefits.
4036: Spirit Walk: Briefly become incorporeal, passing through solid objects or avoiding damage.
4037: Ancestral Guardian: Summon a protective spirit that intercepts damage meant for you.
4038: Elemental Surge: Enhance a totem's effect for one round.
4039: Spirit Binding: Temporarily bind a minor elemental spirit to an object or ally, granting benefits.
4040: 
4041: Level 10+ (Hero Path Selection)
4042: At level 10, shamans choose a Hero Path that reflects their philosophy and methods, further specializing their abilities.
4043: 
4044: Theurgist (Hero Path)
4045: "The elements are not tools to be used, but allies to be respected."
4046: 
4047: Passive Skills
4048: Echo Totem: Automatically cast a second, weaker totem when placing your primary totem.
4049: Elemental Harmony: Your elemental summons gain increased duration and health.
4050: Spiritual Resonance: Totems affect a larger area when placed near each other.
4051: Benevolent Spirits: Your healing effects are 25% more powerful on allies below half health.
4052: 
4053: Active Skills
4054: Summon Ice Wisp: Call forth a spirit of frost that can snare and freeze enemies.
4055: Summon Airling: Conjure an air elemental that stuns enemies and deals lightning damage.
4056: Summon Cinderflame: Create a fire spirit that applies burning damage over time and explodes on death.
4057: Summon Stonekin: Manifest an earth elemental that can root enemies and grant damage reduction to allies.
4058: Elemental Chorus: Ultimate ability that summons all four elemental types around a zone for massive combined effect.
4059: 
4060: Soothsayer (Hero Path)
4061: "The future is written in flame and frost, for those with eyes to see."
4062: 
4063: Passive Skills
4064: Divining Totem: Place a special totem that reveals enemy actions before they occur (one target per round).
4065: Predictive Alchemy: Your flasks have a chance to not be consumed when used.
4066: Fate's Whisper: Gain advantage on one saving throw per encounter.
4067: Balanced Elements: Your elemental effects last longer and have secondary effects.
4068: 
4069: Active Skills
4070: Delayed Detonation: Throw a flask that explodes after a set time or trigger.
4071: Healing Splash: Convert a damage flask into an area healing effect.
4072: Omen Brand: Mark a target so that the next ally's ability against them automatically hits.
4073: Fate Weaving: Manipulate probability to force a reroll of any die.
4074: Omen Loop: Ultimate ability that repeats a spell, flask, or totem cast from two turns ago (once per encounter).
4075: 
4076: Bloodcaller (Hero Path)
4077: "Blood is power. Pain is currency. Death is merely transformation."
4078: 
4079: Upon choosing this path, you permanently transform into a Hagraven, losing traditional spellcasting but gaining powerful new abilities.
4080: 
4081: Passive Skills
4082: Hagraven Form: Your body transforms into a twisted, avian-humanoid hybrid with natural weapons and resistances.
4083: Blood Feast: Heal when enemies near you are bleeding.
4084: Necrotic Aura: Enemies within 10 feet take minor necrotic damage each round.
4085: Spell Resistance: Gain advantage on saves against spells and magical effects.
4086: 
4087: Active Skills
4088: Blight Totem: Place a corrupted totem that reduces all healing in its area by 50%.
4089: Blood Curse: Inflict a bleeding wound that cannot be healed normally.
4090: Talon Strike: Make a vicious attack that causes bleeding and reduces healing received.
4091: Death Shriek: Emit a terrifying cry that causes enemies to have disadvantage on concentration and casting.
4092: Bloodroot Trail: Ultimate ability that leaves a cursed line wherever you move; enemies crossing it take damage and start bleeding.
4093: 
4094: Totem System
4095: 
4096: Shamans may place 1 to 2 active totems (increasing to 2 at level 13), which persist for 3 rounds and grant aura or triggered effects in a 3-tile radius.
4097: 
4098: | Totem Type | Effect |
4099: |------------|--------|
4100: | Totem of Flame | Allies gain +1 fire damage, enemies Burn on critical hits |
4101: | Totem of Stone | Allies gain +1 Damage Reduction, enemies have -1 to shove/push attempts |
4102: | Totem of Frost | Slows enemies entering the aura by 10 feet |
4103: | Totem of Spirit | Enemies take +1 damage from necrotic and hex effects |
4104: | Totem of Renewal | Allies regenerate 2 HP per round while inside the aura |
4105: 
4106: Totems are summoned as a standard action and scale with your Wisdom modifier. You may move a totem as a bonus action once per turn.
4107: 
4108: Flask Bomb System
4109: 
4110: Available only to Theurgists and Soothsayers, the Flask Bomb system allows you to create and throw volatile elemental concoctions as bonus actions.
4111: 
4112: Flask effects include:
4113: Burn: Deal fire damage over time
4114: Stun: Briefly incapacitate targets
4115: Root: Prevent movement
4116: Weaken: Reduce target's damage output
4117: Heal: Restore health to allies
4118: Restore: Remove negative conditions
4119: 
4120: Damage and effects scale by Flask Tier (I–IV), unlocked at levels 1, 5, 11, and 17. The specific effects of your flasks are determined by your elemental attunement and Hero Path specialization.
4121: 
4122: Playing a Shaman
4123: 
4124: As a shaman in Decennium Descent, your effectiveness depends on strategic totem placement, resource management, and adapting to battlefield conditions. Unlike pure casters or martial classes, you excel at controlling zones of the battlefield and supporting allies through a combination of elemental effects and spiritual power.
4125: 
4126: Combat Strategy
4127: Your approach to combat will vary based on your chosen Hero path:
4128: 
4129: Theurgist Shamans should focus on summoning elemental allies and controlling the battlefield through strategic totem placement, using their elemental summons to disrupt enemy formations and protect vulnerable allies.
4130: 
4131: Soothsayer Shamans excel at support and prediction, using their divination abilities to anticipate enemy actions and their flasks to create advantageous situations for allies.
4132: 
4133: Bloodcaller Shamans transform into front-line combatants, using their Hagraven form to engage enemies directly while deploying blighted totems to weaken healing and recovery.
4134: 
4135: All shamans should remember that positioning is crucial, your totems create zones of influence that can turn the tide of battle when placed correctly.
4136: 
4137: Reputation and Social Consequences
4138: Your choice of Hero path significantly affects how you're perceived in the world of Aethel:
4139: 
4140: Theurgist Shamans are generally respected by elemental entities and nature-focused communities, though more formalized religious institutions may view their practices with suspicion.
4141: 
4142: Soothsayer Shamans often serve as advisors and seers, their ability to glimpse possible futures making them valuable to leaders and communities facing uncertain times.
4143: 
4144: Bloodcaller Shamans are feared and reviled in most civilized areas, their twisted Hagraven form and necromantic practices marking them as dangerous outcasts. They often find acceptance only among the most primitive or corrupt societies.
4145: 
4146: Notable Shaman NPCs in Aethel
4147: 
4148: Elder Karga Stonevoice
4149: An orc Theurgist who serves as the spiritual leader of the Broken Tusk tribe. Her mastery of elemental summons has protected her people from numerous threats, and her fire and earth totems are said to burn with the spirits of the tribe's greatest warriors.
4150: 
4151: Vex the Farseer
4152: A goblin Soothsayer who wanders the trade routes, offering predictions and protective charms to travelers. His uncanny ability to foresee ambushes and natural disasters has saved countless merchant caravans, earning him safe passage through territories that would normally be hostile to his kind.
4153: 
4154: The Crowmother
4155: A human Bloodcaller who dwells in the Blighted Marsh, surrounded by corrupted totems and attended by flocks of carrion birds. Once a respected healer, she turned to blood magic after failing to save her village from plague. Now, she harvests the blood of trespassers to fuel rituals aimed at resurrecting her lost people, regardless of what form they might take.
4156: 
4157: Soldier
4158: 
4159: "The human soldier stood resolute at the entrance to the narrow pass, her heavy repeating crossbow loaded and ready. With practiced efficiency, she deployed a wooden palisade across half the path, creating a chokepoint. As the first wave of goblins charged, she activated her overwatch, her eyes scanning the battlefield with cold precision. The lead goblin fell to her first bolt, while the second found itself pinned against the palisade. Behind her, the party's mage began casting, secure in the knowledge that nothing would reach him while the soldier held the line."
4160: 
4161: Soldiers are the battlefield's anchors, ranged tanks clad in heavy armor, wielding repeating crossbows and deploying defensive structures to lock down space, delay advances, and protect vulnerable allies. In the world of Aethel, these tactical experts operate from midrange, relying on intercepting fire, fortification, and strategic deterrence to control the flow of battle.
4162: 
4163: Class Overview
4164: 
4165: As a soldier, you excel at battlefield control and ally protection through a combination of strategic positioning, defensive structures, and precise ranged attacks. Your specialty lies not in dealing massive damage, but in disrupting enemy movements, denying flanking opportunities, and creating safe zones for your more vulnerable companions.
4166: 
4167: In Aethel, soldiers might serve as elite guards for nobility, defensive specialists in mercenary companies, tactical advisors for military forces, or personal protectors for wealthy merchants or spellcasters. Their ability to control battlefield space makes them invaluable in both offensive and defensive operations.
4168: 
4169: Core Mechanics
4170: 
4171: Palisade System – Deploy wooden barriers on the battlefield that block movement and line of sight, creating chokepoints and defensive positions. These structures can be upgraded to provide additional benefits to allies.
4172: 
4173: Overwatch System – Declare a zone that you monitor, allowing you to react with immediate fire when enemies enter the area or threaten your allies within it.
4174: 
4175: Intercept & Brace Mechanics – Use your positioning and reactions to protect allies from attacks and provide defensive bonuses to those behind your line.
4176: 
4177: Primary Attributes
4178: Main Attribute: Constitution (for DR, resistances, control effects)
4179: Secondary Attribute: Dexterity (for ranged accuracy, overwatch utility)
4180: Hit Points: High (10 + Constitution modifier at 1st level, 1d10 per level thereafter)
4181: Armor Proficiency: All armor types, including heavy armor
4182: Weapon Proficiency: Repeating heavy crossbows, tower shields, and compatible sidearms
4183: Tool Proficiency: Carpenter's tools, siege engineer's kit
4184: 
4185: Progression Path
4186: 
4187: Level 1-2 (Base Soldier Skills)
4188: Foundational skills before Arch class selection.
4189: 
4190: Passive Skills
4191: Overwatch: Set up a reaction shot against movement in your designated zone.
4192: Shield Trained: Wield a shield alongside your crossbow and gain +1 Damage Reduction when stationary.
4193: Tactical Awareness: Gain advantage on perception checks related to battlefield threats.
4194: Defensive Positioning: Gain +1 AC when adjacent to terrain or structures.
4195: Steady Aim: Ignore partial cover when making ranged attacks.
4196: 
4197: Active Skills
4198: Covering Fire: Fire a volley that forces enemies in a line to take cover or suffer damage.
4199: Quick Deploy: Set up a temporary barricade that provides partial cover.
4200: Warning Shot: Fire a deterrent shot that imposes disadvantage on an enemy's next attack.
4201: Tactical Reposition: Move half your speed without provoking opportunity attacks.
4202: Rally Point: Designate a location that grants allies who move there a small defensive bonus.
4203: 
4204: Level 3-9 (Arch Class: Commander)
4205: At level 3, soldiers specialize into the Commander arch class, enhancing their tactical control of the battlefield.
4206: 
4207: Passive Skills
4208: Command Step: Once per round, shift one adjacent ally 1 tile in any direction.
4209: Formation Lock: Allies adjacent to you cannot be flanked.
4210: Iron Orders: All allies within 3 tiles gain +1 Save vs Fear and +1 AC.
4211: Palisade Deployment: Deploy a 2x1 barrier that blocks movement and line of sight (level 5).
4212: Intercepting Shot: Use your reaction to fire at an enemy about to hit an ally within 5 tiles (level 6).
4213: Fortify Zone: Enemies in a 3x3 area take –2 to ranged attacks against your allies (level 9).
4214: 
4215: Active Skills
4216: Tactical Assessment: Analyze the battlefield to gain information about enemy weaknesses.
4217: Coordinated Maneuver: Direct an ally to move and take an action out of turn.
4218: Suppression Fire: Create a zone that enemies must save against or take damage when entering.
4219: Defensive Formation: Arrange allies in an optimal pattern, granting defensive bonuses.
4220: Hold the Line: Establish a defensive position that grants bonuses to allies behind you.
4221: 
4222: Level 10+ (Hero Path Selection)
4223: At level 10, soldiers choose a Hero Path that reflects their philosophy and methods, further specializing their abilities.
4224: 
4225: Champion (Hero Path)
4226: "No one falls while I stand."
4227: 
4228: Passive Skills
4229: Protective Aura: Nearby allies ignore fear and charm effects while you stand.
4230: Selfless Guardian: Take reduced damage when intercepting attacks meant for allies.

4233:

4238: Rallying Cry: Remove fear effects from nearby allies and grant temporary hit points.
4239: Sacrificial Defense: Take damage in place of an ally, converting a portion to non-lethal damage.
4240: 
4241: Artillerist (Hero Path)
4242: "Every shot has a purpose. Every bolt finds its mark."
4243:

4246: Tactical Reload: Reload your crossbow as a free action once per round.

4248: Precision Engineering: Your deployed structures have increased durability and effectiveness.
4249:
